# bro GUI parity checklist

This is the master list of everything the ratatui TUI (`crates/bro-tui`) does that the GPU GUI must match. It was built from the five inventories in `docs/gui/inventory/` (01 app core, 02 sidebar/chrome/theme, 03 overlays/launcher, 04 views/panes, 05 sessions/services).
Tick `[x]` only when the GUI does it **and** it was verified in the running app. Use `[~]` for partial and `[-]` for deliberately different, and write the reason into the item.
IDs are `PREFIX-nnn`, numbered within each section and never reused: append new items at the end of their section (`DD-nnn` are the design deltas at the end). `(must)` marks items from an inventory's "must not miss" list; `(quirk)` marks TUI quirks to keep or fix on purpose.
`src:` paths are relative to `crates/bro-tui/src/` unless they start with `bro-core/` or `bro-bridge/` (meaning `crates/<crate>/src/`). Sizes given in cells, cols or rows are TUI cell units: the GUI keeps a virtual cell grid or rescales every such constant (see LAY-002).

---

## LOOP: event loop, timing, focus, housekeeping

### Loop and deadlines
- [x] LOOP-001 — One event channel: everything arrives as `pane::Event` on one mpsc channel; the GUI forwards it into winit through a Sender wrapper that pokes an `EventLoopProxy`, and adds its own input variant (must) · src: pane.rs:17-59 · window: gui/mod.rs run(): one mpsc channel; the "bro-gui-events" thread forwards every pane::Event to winit as UserEvent::App through the EventLoopProxy; UserEvent::Wake is the window's own variant
- [x] LOOP-002 — Pass order: draw, then `recv_timeout(next_deadline)`, handle, drain further events for at most 12 ms, then `after_events`, then check quit · src: app/mod.rs:792-836 · window: winit hands over every pending event (user_event / window_event → App::handle), then about_to_wait runs App::after_events, checks quit and asks for the frame (wakes are coalesced per pane, so no 12 ms cap is needed); fixed: mouse handlers asked winit for the frame directly and Windows painted it before about_to_wait, i.e. before after_events (a grown tile snapped back a frame late); Gui::redraw now only marks the window dirty and about_to_wait asks after the housekeeping (gui/mod.rs)
- [x] LOOP-003 — `next_deadline` base is 30 s, never below 4 ms · src: app/mod.rs:891-936 · window: Gui::next_wake starts from App::next_deadline (30 s base, 4 ms floor), 16 ms while something animates
- [x] LOOP-004 — Deadline 125 ms while any pane is Working, the Watchdog overlay is open, a watchdog is running, or an animated theme shows on the welcome screen · src: app/mod.rs:891-936 · window: shared App::next_deadline; the window's spinners also set ui.ticking
- [x] LOOP-005 — Deadline 110 ms while rain is on or the theme backdrop is animated · src: app/mod.rs:891-936 · window: shared App::next_deadline (110 ms for rain / animated backdrops)
- [x] LOOP-006 — Deadline at toast expiry + 10 ms · src: app/mod.rs:891-936 · window: shared deadline; toasts expire in App::after_events
- [x] LOOP-007 — Deadline for the sidebar scrollbar fade (redraw every 40 ms while fading) · src: app/mod.rs:891-936, app/side_view.rs:19-54 · window: shared deadline (side_view::bar_wake)
- [x] LOOP-008 — Deadline 16 ms while any card is turning · src: app/mod.rs:917-920 · window: shared 16 ms deadline; chrome.rs draw_card also sets ui.ticking while a card turns
- [x] LOOP-009 — Deadlines for the alt-hint wake (300 ms hold) and the screensaver wake · src: app/mod.rs:891-936 · window: shared alt_wake / screensaver_wake; ModifiersChanged (alt alone) sends Event::Alt
- [x] LOOP-010 — Each pane's `tick_every` feeds the deadline: terminals always (400 ms, hidden ones too), other panes only while visible · src: app/mod.rs:891-936, panes/term.rs:1068-1080 · window: shared tick_every (terminals always, others while visible)
- [x] LOOP-011 — `Event::Tick`: run `pump_resumes`, then poll every pane whose `tick_every` elapsed · src: app/mod.rs:995-1008 · window: Gui::tick sends Event::Tick on ResumeTimeReached, and from about_to_wait once the deadline has passed (so constant output can't starve it); App::handle runs pump_resumes then the due polls
- [x] LOOP-012 — `Event::Wake`: per-pane redraw signal coalesced by a pending flag to one outstanding wake per pane; acknowledge, then poll that pane, so output floods never queue redraws ahead of input (must) · src: app/mod.rs:991-994, pane.rs:120-157 · window: shared Waker/WakeSignal coalescing; App::handle acknowledges then polls; the window marks itself dirty
- [x] LOOP-013 — `after_events` runs after every batch in this exact order: screensaver step, drop priority grow if focus moved, follow_tab, prune locks of closed panes, follow_focus, record_view (history), `svc.set_focused_session(sid)`, reap, track_agents, tick_watchdogs, update_insights, pump_voice, toast expiry · src: app/mod.rs:839-859 · window: Gui::after_events calls App::after_events unchanged, then the mirrors' OSC 52 / bell requests, the media gallery and panel requests
- [x] LOOP-014 — Key Release events are ignored; Repeat is handled exactly like Press · src: app/mod.rs:950 · window: input::key returns None for releases and marks repeats KeyEventKind::Repeat; App::handle treats Repeat as Press
- [x] LOOP-015 — Window resize has no special handler: the next layout pass uses the new size and visible PTYs follow their new rects · src: app/mod.rs:989 · window: WindowEvent::Resized resizes the surface and draws at once; layout uses the new size and chrome.rs draw_terminal resizes visible ptys
- [x] LOOP-016 — `Event::Services`: offer Setup once, refresh the open launcher's data and Continue list (see OVL) · src: app/mod.rs:1013-1022 · window: shared App::handle(Services)
- [x] LOOP-017 — `Event::BridgeStarted`: re-register every terminal with the bridge (program = harness label or "shell", no args) · src: panes/term.rs:496 · window: shared (Term::reregister)
- [x] LOOP-018 — `Event::OpenLink`: open on a background thread; an error becomes a toast · src: app/mod.rs:1034-1043 · window: shared thread + error toast; gui/mod.rs app_event also refuses file: links to programs
- [x] LOOP-019 — `Event::OpenProject` (another `bro` started in a folder): ring for attention (GUI: flash the taskbar / request attention instead of BEL), `add_project`, leave the sidebar · src: app/mod.rs:1066-1076 · window: gui/mod.rs app_event: OpenProject is handled by the window (request_user_attention, add_project, leave_sidebar) instead of App::handle, which wrote a BEL to stdout (DD-009)
- [x] LOOP-020 — Startup: every keymap error becomes an Error toast · src: app/mod.rs:382-384 · window: App::new toasts every keymap error (the window builds App::new)
- [x] LOOP-021 — Shutdown: set `quitting`, close every pane (keeps orchestrator state for next start), `svc.shutdown()` (proxy shutdown; bridge shutdown waits up to 5 s), then restore fonts/terminal · src: app/mod.rs:881-888 · window: run() → Gui::shutdown: prefs saved, App::shutdown (quitting, close every pane, svc.shutdown); the window now hides as the loop exits (gui/mod.rs exiting) so the bridge's wait doesn't leave it frozen on screen; restoring fonts/terminal doesn't apply

### Window focus semantics
- [x] LOOP-022 — FocusLost: clear the sidebar filter and alt state, end a sidebar edge drag and save the width, cancel a divider drag, set `term_focused=false` · src: app/mod.rs:978-988 · window: Focused(false) → App FocusLost (filter, alt, term_focused) plus the window's alt_alone / drag / pressing; fixed: a sidebar or media-panel edge drag cut short now saves its width (gui/mod.rs window_event)
- [x] LOOP-023 — (quirk) FocusLost does not cancel a tile-edge drag: decide keep or fix · src: app/mod.rs:978-988 · window: decided: fix; the window ends every drag when it loses focus, a held tile edge too (gui/mod.rs window_event Focused)
- [x] LOOP-024 — FocusGained sets `term_focused` and counts as a touch (wakes the screensaver) · src: app/mod.rs:974-977 · window: Focused(true) → App FocusGained: term_focused + touched()
- [x] LOOP-025 — (must) Window focus feeds attention: `seen = visible && window focused`; a session that finishes while the window is unfocused counts as unseen (done mark, toast, recap timer); use winit `Focused` · src: app/sessions.rs:766-799 · window: winit Focused drives App.term_focused, which track_agents and update_insights read for "seen"

### Focus model
- [x] LOOP-026 — `focused()` = `stack_focus` (or the first stacked pane) while stacked, else `tabs[cur].focus` · src: app/mod.rs:410-415 · window: shared App::focused
- [x] LOOP-027 — `visible()` = the stack (only the focus when `stack_zoom`), else the zoomed focus, else every leaf of the current tab · src: app/mod.rs:727-736 · window: shared App::visible
- [x] LOOP-028 — follow_focus: when focus moves to a non-orchestrator session, `cur_project` becomes its project; `pick_project` holds a chosen project until focus moves again · src: app/mod.rs:863-878 · window: shared follow_focus / pick_project
- [x] LOOP-029 — current_project, first match wins: sidebar selection while browsing, `cur_project`, the focused non-orchestrator session's project, the first opened project; new sessions and bridge creates without a cwd start here · src: app/sessions.rs:430-441 · window: shared current_project
- [x] LOOP-030 — `run_act` leaves the sidebar whenever the action opened an overlay · src: app/commands.rs:205-207 · window: shared run_act tail
- [x] LOOP-031 — `svc.set_focused_session(sid)` every batch (voice remembers what you were looking at) · src: app/mod.rs:851-852 · window: shared after_events

### Navigation history (mouse back/forward)
- [x] LOOP-032 — Entries `Seen::{Pane(id), Grid(project, id), NewSession}`, capped at 200, recorded after every event batch; duplicates dropped; a new view truncates forward entries · src: app/history.rs:12-48,63-76 · window: shared History; record_view in after_events
- [x] LOOP-033 — A launcher closed without launching (back to the previous entry) is erased from history · src: app/history.rs:36-41 · window: shared History::record
- [x] LOOP-034 — navigate(back/forward): any non-launcher overlay just closes and nothing else happens · src: app/history.rs:87-139 · window: shared App::navigate
- [x] LOOP-035 — Otherwise record the current view, then step to the nearest acceptable entry; inside your own unzoomed manual stack only stacked panes are accepted, no grids · src: app/history.rs:87-139 · window: shared App::navigate
- [x] LOOP-036 — Grid entries re-show that project grid or All, then focus; Pane entries focus and re-zoom if you were full screen and the tab has splits; NewSession entries reopen the launcher · src: app/history.rs:87-139 · window: shared App::navigate
- [x] LOOP-037 — (must) Input: mouse X1/X2 (winit `MouseButton::Back/Forward`) and browser Back/Forward keys on every platform (TUI had them on Windows only) · src: console_nav.rs:30-73 · window: mouse Back/Forward buttons → Event::Navigate (gui/mouse.rs pressed); fixed: the keyboard's BrowserBack / BrowserForward keys were dropped by input::key, now gui/mod.rs gui_key sends Event::Navigate on every platform

### Draw/input contract
- [x] LOOP-038 — (must) Hit-testing reads the last layout (immediate mode: `side_hits`, `pane_close`, `pane_minimize`, `outer`, `inner`, `confirm_hits`, `card_links`, `strip`, launcher/resume `hits`); the GUI keeps the same "last layout" contract · src: app/mouse.rs:12-451 · window: input reads the last frame: Gui.hits, Gui.layout, overlay_grid, painted, plus App.outer/inner/body set by layout::panes
- [x] LOOP-039 — (must) Move draw-time side effects into the layout/update pass: `body`, `side_area`, `strip`, `strip_sel` clamp, `drawn_tab`, `side_hits`, `outer`, `inner`, `tile_regions`, `grown`, `card_links`, `flips.retain`, selection clear, copy + toast, `side_sel` clamp · src: app/draw.rs, app/side_view.rs:137 · window: draw_all / layout::panes set body, side_area, strip, drawn_tab, outer, inner, tile_regions, grown before painting; chrome.rs keeps flips.retain and card_links; sidebar.rs clamps side_sel; fixed in gui/mod.rs: strip_sel cleared while the strip is off, and a session that left the screen loses its selection after the frame
- [~] LOOP-040 — (must) Pane render can return Actions that App applies mid-draw (open/close panes) and sends a Tick; the GUI must apply them outside the paint · src: app/draw.rs:434-437 · partial: chrome.rs draw_card applies a fallback view's render Actions (app.apply + Tick) in the middle of the frame; collect them in Painted and apply them in Gui::frame_inner after draw_all (integrator: chrome.rs + mod.rs)
- [x] LOOP-041 — (must) Pending clipboard reads are cancelled by any key, click or paste (see KEY paste) and a late result never lands in a newly opened popup · src: app/input.rs:17-31, app/mouse.rs:13-15 · window: App::key / App::paste and gui/mouse.rs pressed clear clipboard_request; App::handle(ClipboardText) checks the token
- [x] LOOP-042 — `hover` is updated by every mouse event (move included) and drives all hover effects · src: app/mouse.rs:13-23 · window: CursorMoved updates Gui.pointer, which every hover fade and the pointer shape read; also fixed here (CHR-043): mouse moves, clicks, wheel, drops and IME text now count as a touch (gui/mod.rs window_event), and a click or wheel that wakes the screensaver does only that

### Mouse routing
- [-] LOOP-043 — Precedence: open overlay rules; sidebar edge drag in progress; tile-edge drag in progress; sidebar edge press; wheel over strip/sidebar; sidebar and strip hits; blank sidebar; pane minimize then close buttons; divider grab then tile-edge press; divider drag/selection drag/release; card-back link press; pane content (link, ctrl grow, prompt option, double-click, selection start); focus; link press; passthrough · src: app/mouse.rs:12-451 · deliberately different: DD-029; the rest of the mouse order follows the hit list (gui/mouse.rs)
- [~] LOOP-044 — Button-down, drag and wheel clear a pending link press · src: app/mouse.rs:13-23 · partial: gui/mouse.rs pressed clears Gui.link_press, but a drag (moved while pressing) and the wheel don't; clear it in mouse.rs moved (while pressing) and wheel
- [~] LOOP-045 — Any button-down on a pane focuses it and leaves the sidebar · src: app/mouse.rs:436-443 · partial: body presses focus with any button, but gui/mouse.rs pane_press (Header) returns before focus() for a right click; focus first for every button
- [x] LOOP-046 — Drags (sidebar edge, split divider, tile edge, selection) capture the mouse across panes until release · src: app/mouse.rs:115-151,308-349 · window: Gui.drag owns the gesture until release whatever is under the pointer (winit captures the mouse while a button is down)

## TERM: terminal panes

### Spawn and environment
- [x] TERM-001 — Lazy spawn: a terminal spawns at its first layout, at the real pane cell size (TUI: `ensure_started` in render) · src: panes/term.rs:232, panes/term.rs:1102-1122 · window: chrome.rs draw_terminal: term.ensure_started at term/draw.rs fit() of the card's content on its first frame
- [x] TERM-002 — `start_now` for hidden/remote panes and queued resumes: spawn at body size minus 2 (minimum 10x40, as in source) · src: app/sessions.rs:168 · window: shared start_now on App.body (the window's virtual cells are terminal cells)
- [x] TERM-003 — PTY via `portable-pty` (vendored, with the job-handle hook) · src: panes/term.rs:347-407 · window: shared panes/term.rs (the window's sessions are the same Term)
- [x] TERM-004 — `.cmd`/`.bat` programs run through `cmd.exe /c` (`resolve()`) · src: panes/term.rs:913 · window: shared resolve()
- [x] TERM-005 — cwd falls back to the home dir when it isn't a directory · src: panes/term.rs:347-407 · window: shared Term::start
- [x] TERM-006 — (must) Env set: `TERM=xterm-256color`, `COLORTERM=truecolor`, `BRO=1`, `BRO_SESSION_ID=<sid>`, `BRO_BIN=<console bro.exe>`, `BRO_BRIDGE_URL` (removed when the bridge is off) · src: panes/term.rs:355-371 · window: shared Term::start (BRO_BIN is the bro.exe the window runs as)
- [x] TERM-007 — Env removed: `NO_COLOR` and every `CLAUDE_SESSION_ENV` var; then the spec's `env_remove`, then its `env` · src: panes/term.rs:31-42 · window: shared Term::start
- [x] TERM-008 — Windows: `bro_core::proc_tree::ProcessTree` job object via `set_windows_job_handle`, then `tree.watch_pty(pid)` · src: panes/term.rs:347-407 · window: shared Term::start
- [x] TERM-009 — After spawn: `svc.bridge_register(SessionMeta)`, keep the Spawn (for `/recap`), start an Insights worker for Claude/Codex outside demo · src: panes/term.rs:400-404,479-493 · window: shared Term::start
- [x] TERM-010 — Writer: one "bro-pty-input" thread per pane fed by a channel; order kept; the UI never blocks on a child that stops reading · src: panes/writer.rs · window: shared panes/writer.rs
- [x] TERM-011 — Reader thread per pane, per chunk: parse, answer DSR, `svc.bridge_output(sid, bytes)` raw and in order, update `last_output`, wake; on EOF set `exited` and wake · src: panes/term.rs:503-542 · window: shared start_reader (also feeds the mirror)
- [x] TERM-012 — (must) Keep the raw PTY byte stream: own reader (portable-pty + job object) feeds both alacritty's parser and `bridge_output`; alacritty's own EventLoop hides raw bytes · src: panes/term.rs:503-542 · window: start_reader feeds vt100, the alacritty mirror (gui/term/mirror.rs ScreenMirror::feed) and bridge_output from the same chunk, in order
- [x] TERM-013 — DSR `\x1b[6n` is answered (ConPTY blocks until it is), after releasing the parser lock; with alacritty, answer via its `PtyWrite` event · src: panes/term.rs:503-542 · window: the session answers CPR after releasing the parser lock; gui/term/mirror.rs Listener drops alacritty's own CPR (is_cursor_report) and writes its other answers (DA, colours, kitty) back
- [-] TERM-014 — `Term::fixed`: static ANSI content for demo snapshots (never spawns) · src: panes/term.rs:324 · n/a: fixed panes only exist with Opts.fixed_demo (tests); the window's demo runs live shells. If the window ever shows one: Term::fixed (panes/term.rs) must also resize + feed the mirror, or it draws blank

### Rendering
- [x] TERM-015 — The PTY is resized to the pane's inner rect whenever layout changes; hidden and folded panes keep their last size · src: panes/term.rs:1102-1122 · window: chrome.rs draw_terminal resizes visible, unfolded panes every frame (Term::resize → apply_size → mirror resize); hidden and folded ones keep theirs
- [x] TERM-016 — Painted themes supply the session's default fg/bg and the 16 ANSI colours (alacritty colour config) (must) · src: panes/term.rs:929-954 · window: gui/term/draw.rs Palette from the theme's Tokens (term_fg, term_bg, ansi); the mirror's Env answers OSC 10/11/4 with the same colours
- [x] TERM-017 — Light themes: program white text becomes theme fg, dark shading becomes light tints (must) · src: panes/term.rs:929-954 · window: gui/term/draw.rs Palette::resolve: SGR 40–47 / 90–107 colours went through `named`, which painted a white background in the theme's text colour and left black backgrounds dark on light themes; they now take the indexed rules (white text → text colour, black background → light tint). Test
- [x] TERM-018 — Dark painted themes: greys are recast between the theme's bg and fg (must) · src: panes/term.rs:929-954 · window: gui/term/draw.rs Palette::resolve: 232–255 and grey RGB recast between the theme's bg and fg on painted dark themes
- [x] TERM-019 — Cursor drawn at the program's cursor position honouring its visibility; nothing on a card back or a folded sliver · src: panes/term.rs:1102-1122 · window: gui/term/draw.rs draw: cursor at the program's position when SHOW_CURSOR, not Hidden, not scrolled back; card backs and folded cards never draw a screen
- [x] TERM-020 — Session grid uses a monospace font (one of the bundled fonts, see THM); wide (CJK/emoji) cells take 2 columns · src: fonts.rs · window: the terminal font from crate::fonts (Gui::sync_font); WIDE_CHAR cells drawn over two columns
- [~] TERM-021 — (must) Provide a "screen rows as plain strings" accessor over the grid for `scan`, `permission_prompt`, `prompt_option_at`, auto-resume `screen_stall`, `early_failure` and `screen_urls` · src: panes/term.rs:798-826 · partial: vt100 still parses in the window, so scan, permission_prompt, prompt_option_at, screen_stall and early_failure work; screen_urls() reads Term.links, which only Term::render fills, so card backs lose on-screen URLs. Fix in panes/term.rs screen_urls (detect from the vt100 screen when a mirror is set) or chrome.rs draw_back (use PaneState.links)

- [x] TERM-035 — (must) `target` whitelist: http/https (with a host), mailto, file only; reject > 8192 chars and control chars (no `javascript:` / `data:`) · src: panes/links.rs:38-50 · window: links::target for OSC 8 and web_urls; links::open checks again

- [x] TERM-037 — Locations `:L`, `:L:C`, `#L12`, `#L12C3`, `(L,C)`; file links become `file://` URIs with `#L42C3` fragments · src: panes/links.rs:13-35 · window: shared FileCache lookup → links::file_uri with #L/#LC
- [x] TERM-038 — Only paths the file cache confirms become links · src: panes/file_cache.rs:160-174 · window: shared Lookup::get answers only confirmed paths

## LAY: layout

### Units and the layout pass
- [x] LAY-001 — (must) Explicit layout pass before input: (model, body) → {outer, inner, regions, groups, folded, column, grown}, stored as the "last layout" read by hit-testing, alt+arrows, Split direction and tile edges (TUI computed it inside draw, so before the first draw alt+arrows did nothing and Split fell back to a 0×0 body and split Down) · src: app/draw.rs:216-306 · window: gui/layout.rs `panes` runs before drawing each frame and writes body/outer/inner/tile_regions/grown; the window only shows after the first frame, so no input meets a 0×0 body
- [x] LAY-002 — (must) Layout math is in cells with a 2.2 cell aspect: keep a virtual cell grid or rescale every constant (COMFORT 102×32, USEFUL 32×7, FOLDED 10×2, LOCK_MIN 16×5, frame ring, 14×6 frame min, 72-col sidebar cutoff, sidebar 26..half, column 20/44/96, button/hint minimums 5/24/12; lock `at` in cells from body start) · src: layout.rs, priority.rs · window: virtual cell grid at the terminal font's cell (layout.rs `panes`, `Layout::to_cells/to_px`); px constants stand in for the cell ones (sidebar cutoff 640, sidebar 220..w/2, × at >160, hints ≥160×90); lock `at` in cells from body start (mouse.rs TileEdge) (DD-003)
- [x] LAY-003 — `layout.rs` and `priority.rs` are pure and deterministic; reuse them over a neutral rect type · src: layout.rs, priority.rs · window: layout.rs reuses crate::layout (rects, group_regions, stack_rects) and crate::priority (lock, prioritize, want) unchanged

### Chords and overrides
- [x] KEY-061 — Normalise: mask to CTRL/ALT/SHIFT (SUPER, META, HYPER dropped, so Cmd/Super can never be bound); ASCII uppercase → lowercase + SHIFT; ctrl+@ = ctrl+space; non-letter non-space chars drop SHIFT; BackTab drops SHIFT and Tab+SHIFT becomes BackTab · src: keymap.rs:415-440 · window: shared Chord::new; Super chords never reach the workspace (gui/input.rs)
- [x] KEY-062 — Parse: modifier aliases `ctrl|control|c`, `alt|meta|opt|option|m`, `shift|s`; trailing `++` = the plus key · src: keymap.rs:447-496 · window: shared Chord::parse
- [x] KEY-063 — Named keys `space|spc`, `enter|return|ret`, `tab`, `backtab`, `esc|escape`, `backspace|bs`, `delete|del`, `insert|ins`, `home`, `end`, `pageup|pgup`, `pagedown|pgdn`, `up|down|left|right`, `f1..f24`; any single character · src: keymap.rs:447-496 · window: shared Chord::parse

- [x] KEY-070 — 2: paste chords (ctrl+v, ctrl+shift+v, shift+insert), prefix not armed, start an async clipboard read and stop, everywhere, terminals and overlays included (must) · src: app/input.rs:18-31 · window: shared App::key (ctrl+v, ctrl+shift+v, shift+insert arrive as such from gui/input.rs)
- [x] KEY-071 — 3: an open overlay takes every key (no global chords, no prefix) · src: app/input.rs:124-201 · window: shared App::key; only the window's own keys (font size, copy, F11, browser back/forward in gui_key) come first

- [x] KEY-073 — 5: alt+v on a focused terminal (prefix not armed, sidebar not focused) grabs image/files (see below) · src: app/input.rs:14-108 · window: shared alt+v grab; gui/mod.rs app_event also adds the picture to the media gallery
- [x] KEY-074 — 6: prefix armed handling; 7: the prefix key arms it · src: app/input.rs:14-108 · window: shared App::key

- [x] KEY-076 — 9: direct chords; 10: the strip if it has the keys; 11: the sidebar if it has the keys; 12: no panes → welcome keys · src: app/input.rs:14-108 · window: shared App::key
- [x] KEY-077 — 13: a flipped focused card turns face up (key still delivered); 14: the pane's own handler · src: app/input.rs:92-95 · window: shared App::key
- [x] KEY-078 — A non-terminal pane that doesn't use the key: `?` opens Help, Esc focuses the sidebar on the focused row · src: app/input.rs:98-106 · window: shared App::key

Window additions on purpose (DD-013 spirit): buttons on Resume, Confirm, File and AgentFiles; the wheel scrolls the watchdog card's body (OVL-226) and the prompt editor's text; the prompt editor takes button and caret clicks (TUI: no mouse, OVL-234); sideways wheel still missing (OVL-177)
- [-] OVL-004 — Palette and Help close on any button-down anywhere, inside included (palette restores the theme); no wheel, no row clicks (must) · src: app/mouse.rs:27-112 · deliberately different: DD-005: app/gui/mouse.rs `pressed` closes Palette/Help only on their scrim (`Action::CloseOverlay`, any button; the palette restores `theme_before`); inside, palette rows select and the selected one runs (palette.rs `click`), the wheel moves the palette's selection and scrolls the keys sheet (palette.rs / help.rs `wheel`); checked in pal-click1, pal-wheel, pal-outside shots

- [~] OVL-092 — Filesystem lookups run off the render thread in the GUI (TUI did sync I/O per keystroke and in draw) (must) · src: folder.rs:58-74 · partial: Draw-time stats are off the UI thread (folder/gui.rs typed_state stats on a worker thread and caches the answer). But FolderPicker::refresh still runs expand(f).is_dir() and complete()'s read_dir on every keystroke, Tab and paste, and open_selected runs fs::metadata on Enter or click. All of these run on the window's event thread through Launcher::key. Fix in folder.rs: refresh/complete/open_selected need a worker with a request id (like create_project_folder) and an Event that applies the result.

Esc clears the filter, then closes back to the list (project_outcome Close). - [x] SYS-016 — `~/.bro/v2-instance.json` holds `{pid, port, token}`; loopback TCP; line protocol `token\topen\t<dir>`; replies `ok <hwnd>`, `denied`, `closing` · src: instance.rs · window: shared instance.rs (`hand_off` / `serve` / `running`)

Before the window exists the reply is `ok 0` and the client doesn't raise anything
- [x] SYS-019 — The server posts `Event::OpenProject(dir)` (see LOOP); the guard removes the file on exit only if the token is still ours · src: instance.rs · window: `serve` posts Event::OpenProject (`App::handle` → `add_project`, plus `request_user_attention` in gui/mod.rs `app_event`); the Guard lives in `run` until after `Gui::shutdown`. Risk: `App::handle` still writes BEL to stdout on OpenProject; via `bro gui` stdout is NUL, but the bro-gui.exe/CREATE_NO_WINDOW path inherits whatever handles it gets, so the new launcher should pass null stdio (as `window::detach_from_console` does)

### Windows launcher and identity
- [~] SYS-020 — The GUI replaces `bro-app.exe`: windows-subsystem (no console flash), its own taskbar identity (AppUserModelID + relaunch command), icon, and `--new` semantics (bro-app always added `--new`) · src: bin/bro-app.rs · partial: bin/bro-gui.rs: windows subsystem, hidden console for bro.exe (CREATE_NO_WINDOW), embedded icon (checked in bro-gui.exe). Missing: AppUserModelID + relaunch command. Nothing calls `SetCurrentProcessExplicitAppUserModelID` or sets the window's `PKEY_AppUserModel_*`, so a pinned window relaunches bare bro.exe and the launcher's pin doesn't group with the window. Fix in app/gui/window.rs `create` (or `run`, before the window): an explicit AUMID, then on the HWND (`SHGetPropertyStoreForWindow`) ID, RelaunchCommand ("<launcher> <folder>", as bro-app's `relaunch_command`), RelaunchDisplayNameResource "bro", RelaunchIconResource "<bro.exe>,0". `--new`: the launcher doesn't add it (a second launch hands off; `--new` means guest), unlike bro-app's always-`--new`; settle it in the rework

- [x] SYS-022 — bro-app picked the newest-mtime `bro.exe` among sibling, `$CARGO_HOME/bin` and PATH (ties keep the first); the GUI uses the same rule to find the console `bro.exe` for `BRO_BIN` and CLI tabs (must) · src: bin/bro-app.rs · window: bro-app.exe is the window's launcher and keeps find_bro's newest-copy rule (bin/bro-app.rs desktop)

### Perf log
- [~] SYS-032 — Each pass is timed: events (count, worst kind and pane), housekeeping, render, slowest pane render, present/write, input wait (from input arrival to the UI taking it) · src: perf.rs:12-191 · partial: the window times events (`Gui::app_event` → `perf.begin`/`event`), housekeeping (`Gui::after_events`) and the whole frame (`Gui::frame` → `drawn`/`finish`). Missing: input wait (no `perf::input_read`; winit input arrives on the UI thread, so it needs the OS event time), the slowest pane render (chrome.rs `draw_card` never calls `perf.pane_rendered`), and native mouse/wheel/drop handling isn't timed (gui/mouse.rs doesn't go through `perf.event`). Fix: gui/mod.rs `window_event` (time the mouse handlers into the pass) and chrome.rs `draw_card` (`pane_rendered` per card)
- [x] SYS-033 — A pass over 50 ms or input waiting ≥ 50 ms writes one line to `~/.bro/v2-perf.log` on the "bro-perf-log" thread; the log is cut to its newest half past 512 KB · src: perf.rs:12-191 · window: shared `perf::log` via `Pass::finish`, called every frame
- [~] SYS-034 — `kind()` names every Event variant; extend it for GUI input events · src: perf.rs · partial: `kind()` still names every Event; the window's own input (mouse press/release/move/wheel, drops, IME, `gui_key`) bypasses `perf.event`, so a slow click isn't attributed. Fix: a named timing helper in perf.rs (e.g. `Pass::timed("mouse", f)`) used from gui/mod.rs `window_event`

## MED: media sidebar (new for the GUI)

The TUI never draws a file's picture: media, PDFs and binaries are handed straight to the OS default app and the preview closes (`file_view.rs:172-190`, `app/files.rs:102-107`). The GUI shows photos and video in a right-hand sidebar instead. Text files keep the text preview (OVL).

### Sources
- [x] MED-001 — `bro open` on a media file (BridgeCommand::OpenFile) shows it in the media sidebar when you're watching that session (same gating as OVL-158); otherwise it joins the gallery and toasts "{who} opened {name} · {prefix f} to view" · src: app/files.rs:156-169 · window: watching: the file preview's load is intercepted in app/gui/mod.rs app_event (Payload::Media -> media.add Opened + show_media). Not watching: the TUI only toasts "{who} opened {name} · {prefix f} to view"; media/mod.rs collect() now also adds app.last_opened to the gallery (owner = that pane); test a_picture_an_agent_opens_while_you_look_elsewhere_joins_the_gallery
- [x] MED-002 — prefix f (last opened) on a media file opens it in the media sidebar · src: app/files.rs:206-216 · window: prefix f -> App::show_last_opened -> open_file_view -> the same app_event intercept puts it in the panel
- [x] MED-003 — Claude SendUserFile "sent" files with media extensions join the session's gallery with their caption · src: agent_files.rs:82-102, panes/insights.rs · window: media/mod.rs collect(): facts.sent -> Source::Sent with the SendUserFile caption (now at the fact's own time, fact_time())
- [x] MED-004 — Clicking a terminal `file:` link with a media extension opens the media sidebar (not the OS app) · src: app/files.rs:47-53 · window: app/gui/mouse.rs open_link -> App::open_file_link -> file preview -> intercept -> panel; checked in the demo (clicked a fractal.png link: panel opened, "opened by …", keyboard stayed on the session)
- [x] MED-005 — Clicking a media file row on a card back opens the media sidebar · src: app/mouse.rs:351-363 · window: turned-card links -> mouse.rs open_link -> same file-preview intercept (code path)
- [x] MED-006 — Picking a media item in the AgentFiles overlay opens the media sidebar · src: agent_files.rs · window: app/gui/overlays/mod.rs -> App::agent_files_outcome(Open) -> open_file_view -> intercept
- [x] MED-007 — Touched files (write/create/edit/move) with image extensions join the gallery; the entry refreshes when the file's mtime changes · src: agent_files.rs:82-102 · window: collect() skipped Move facts: a move's destination now joins as Made (Read joins as "looked at", Delete skipped). A file rewritten on disk now reloads in place: mtime check in add_seen + new check_changes() poll (selected + newest 12, every 1.5 s, from collect) + refresh() keeps the picture and zoom until the new pixels arrive; test a_file_rewritten_on_disk_loads_again
- [x] MED-008 — alt+v pastes saved to `~/.bro/paste/paste-<ms>.png` join the gallery of the session they were pasted into · src: clip.rs:110-177 · window: app/gui/mod.rs app_event Event::Clipboard(pane, Some(paths)) -> media.add(Pasted, that pane's owner) (not run: alt+v writes the real ~/.bro/paste)
- [x] MED-009 — Paths resolve with the preview's rules (OVL-161) and kind comes from the bridge's `image_mime` / `media_mime` extension tables · src: bro-bridge/file_preview.rs:290-320 · window: any media kind the preview loads, including those it calls binary (tiff, heic, avi, pdf), opens in the panel (gui/mod.rs app_event)
- [x] MED-010 — Loading runs off the UI thread with a request id; stale results are dropped; a spinner shows while loading · src: file_view.rs:162 · window: loads run off the UI thread (media/loader.rs) with a spinner while View::Loading; stale results were dropped by path only (a same-path duplicate replaced the view and leaked its texture): Job/Done now carry a request number (Media.req, bumped in select/refresh) and only the newest is shown; replaced textures are freed

### Security
- [x] MED-043 — (must) Never OS-open executables reached through links or the media panel: block .exe, .msi, .scr, .com, .bat, .cmd, .ps1, .vbs, .js, .jar, .lnk, .reg, .msc, .hta, .cpl, .appx/.msix and similar; show why instead (TUI risk: a clicked `.exe` link is binary, goes to ShellExecute "open" and runs) · src: file_view.rs:172-190, panes/links.rs:154-171 · window: panes::links::open refuses programs for every OS open; test programs_never_open_from_a_link
- [x] MED-044 — (must) Keep the `links::target` whitelist (http, https, mailto, file only; no control chars; ≤ 8192 chars) and pass links as data, never through a shell · src: panes/links.rs:38-50 · window: panes/links.rs target(): http/https with a host, mailto, file only; no control chars; <= 8192; open() passes the URI as data (ShellExecuteW / open / xdg-open argument, no shell)
- [x] MED-045 — Folders still open the file manager through the fallback URI · src: file_view.rs:179-185 · window: folder -> file_view::read Err(no path) -> Outcome::Fallback(uri) -> Event::OpenLink -> app_event (not executable) -> file manager
- [x] MED-046 — Decoders run on worker threads with time and memory limits, so a hostile or huge file can't stall or crash the UI · src: (new) · window: bro-media still.rs: every image decoder now runs under a 1 GiB decode budget (limits(), check_size(), total_bytes check before from_decoder; PngDecoder/GifDecoder ran unlimited: a 75-byte PNG claiming 60000x60000 allocated 14 GB, a 35-byte GIF with a 65535x65535 canvas hung > 2 min), animations stop decoding after 8 s, SVG/svgz size caps; too-large stills fall to WIC (scaled, 42 MB peak). media/loader.rs: each job runs on its own thread under a time limit (25 s images, 45 s players) and a panicking decoder no longer kills a worker. Player keeps its 30 s open limit and bounded frame queue; ffmpeg runs have 20 s limits. Tests a_tiny_file_claiming_a_huge_picture_fails_fast_without_allocating_it, a_stuck_or_crashing_decoder_reports_instead_of_hanging. Also: SVG/svgz parsing is capped at 2M XML nodes (still.rs parse_svg, MAX_SVG_NODES). Before, a 0.9 MB svgz of <g/> (922 MB unpacked) would build a tree of gigabytes; now it's refused with "the SVG is too complex to preview" (checked in the window). Test an_svg_with_too_many_nodes_is_refused_and_svgz_still_reads

---

## Design deltas (intentional differences)

- [-] DD-001 — No accent side bars anywhere: the sidebar's project/current/stack markers (`▍ ▏ │ ┃ ▶`) and every list's selected-row `▌` (launcher, pickers, Continue, Resume, palette, agent files, settings, quick install, profiles, bridge URLs, file-preview target line) become background tint, a full border or an icon · reason: the user's design rule forbids coloured stripes on card/row edges
- [-] DD-002 — Focused pane: a full border in the accent plus a faintly tinted header, not a solid band along the top edge; group frames become a neutral container, not a top rule · reason: same no-accent-bar rule (inventory 02 §6.12)
- [-] DD-003 — Pixel layout for chrome; terminals keep a cell grid; every cell constant (LAY-002) is converted through a virtual cell size · reason: GPU GUI, proportional chrome
- [-] DD-004 — Chrome text in Inter or Poppins; terminal grid, code preview and TextAreas in a monospace font (or with a measure callback) · reason: user's font rule; terminals need monospace
- [-] DD-005 — Palette and Help close on an outside click only; inside, rows are clickable and the wheel scrolls · reason: TUI closed on any click, which is hostile in a GUI (inventory 01 must #10, 03 §6)
- [-] DD-006 — Mouse back/forward and browser keys work on every platform · reason: TUI had them on Windows only
- [-] DD-007 — Media opens in the right-hand media sidebar; the OS app is an explicit action, and executables are never opened · reason: MED; removes the auto-launch risk
- [-] DD-008 — Native OS clipboard instead of OSC 52; native QR from the pairing URL instead of half-block text · reason: no terminal in between
- [-] DD-009 — Instance hand-off flashes the taskbar / requests attention instead of ringing BEL · reason: no console bell in a GUI
- [-] DD-010 — Selection uses a selection colour, not reverse video; copy comes from the terminal grid, not rendered cells · reason: GPU rendering, and card-back text must not leak into copies
- [-] DD-011 — Fonts load directly and the font row is always shown (no OSC 50, no `--app-window` gate) · reason: the GUI owns its renderer
- [-] DD-012 — Hard-coded strings use the live keymap ("alt+b" subtitle, "? keys", "the orchestrator isn't running · alt+O starts it") and stale copy is rewritten (help "tab ← →", help mouse sheet, watchdog intro/Reviewer row, "ctrl+s start" when saving) · reason: fix TUI quirks rather than copy them
- [-] DD-013 — Bridge view gets mouse support; the usage view wheel is clamped; settings hint lines and view hint lines may become clickable buttons · reason: TUI gaps, not intended behaviour
- [-] DD-014 — Sidebar rows get a hover highlight and the scrollbar is draggable; resize grips show a resize cursor · reason: standard GUI affordances the TUI couldn't offer
- [-] DD-015 — Alt hints are placed from layout/hit metadata, not by reading back a rendered buffer · reason: no cell buffer to read
- [-] DD-016 — Clock updates every minute even when idle · reason: TUI could be up to 30 s late because it only redrew on events
- [-] DD-017 — Optional taskbar flash when a session needs you while the window is unfocused; toasts stay the main channel · reason: TUI had no OS notifications; a GUI window can request attention cheaply
- [-] DD-018 — Blocking I/O moves off the render thread (PATH lookup at launcher open, Gemini creds read, folder-picker filesystem calls, `push_known_folders`) · reason: frame pacing in a GPU loop
- [-] DD-019 — Window title and taskbar identity (AppUserModelID, relaunch command, icon) replace bro-app.exe and Windows Terminal app mode · reason: the GUI is its own window
- [-] DD-020 — WinUI 3 geometry everywhere: 4px corners on controls, list rows and chips, 8px on cards, dialogs and the session cards; the sessions sit on a content layer with a rounded top-left corner · reason: the user's design direction ("less round corners, more WinUI 3, keeping cross platform")
- [-] DD-021 — No identity dots or count badges in lists: a project is its name in its own colour, with no colour dot or folder icon; project rows drop "N live"; session status is an icon (spinner, alert, check), idle shows nothing; the proxy/bridge/welcome status dots become check / spinner / cross icons · reason: the user's direction ("dont use the colored dots … or the '2 live' badges")
- [-] DD-022 — Agents, shells and providers show their real logos (Claude, OpenAI for Codex, PowerShell, Bash, Gemini, OpenRouter…) instead of glyphs; Pi and omp keep a glyph · reason: the user's direction ("Claude should be the claude icon logo")
- [-] DD-023 — No icon on a coloured tile anywhere (settings section icons, toast icons, mark tiles) · reason: the user's direction
- [-] DD-024 — The session header shows the activity indicator alone (no "working" / "needs you" text badge; the word is a tooltip); header badges (watchdog, resume, remote size, scrollback) are quiet coloured text, not pills · reason: the user's direction ("just the working indicator")
- [-] DD-025 — Usage left is drawn as little segments (the terminal UI's ▰▰▱) in the sidebar, the usage view and the pickers · reason: the user's direction
- [-] DD-026 — Holding alt shows WinUI key tips (small raised boxes over the thing each key reaches) and a key bar on each card; the static key labels they would cover hide meanwhile · reason: the user's direction ("holding Alt still shows the hotkeys in a great style")
- [-] DD-027 — The project tabs are a WinUI TabView: the showing tab is filled like the content and open into it, others are labels with hover fills and separators, + follows the last tab · reason: the user's direction
- [-] DD-028 — The launcher's project is an address bar across the top (folder, path segments, chevron); clicking it opens the project picker · reason: the user's direction
- [-] DD-029 — In a terminal, a double-click selects a word and a triple-click a line (as in every terminal window); a double-click on a session's header still zooms it · reason: a window's terminals copy by selection, which the terminal UI left to its host terminal
- [-] DD-030 — alt+m shows and hides the media panel without taking the keyboard; clicking the panel gives it its keys (esc gives them back) · reason: letters typed for a session must never trigger panel actions (o opens, del removes)
- [-] DD-031 — Themed title decorations (brackets, upper case, fullwidth letters, inverted title tabs) aren't drawn; a theme's colours, backdrop and screen effects are · reason: the WinUI header keeps one calm style across themes (DESIGN.md: no all-caps)
- [-] DD-032 — Focus mode (alt+f, Act::Focus `focus_mode`, rebindable, in help and the palette) is the window's: Claude Code and Codex sessions show their conversation (`bro_core::sessions::conversation`), a reply box and the choice on screen as buttons, with the session running on under it; the terminal UI toasts where it is · reason: the user's direction (a big-type message and response view that keeps the harness out of sight), which a grid of cells can't draw
